## `ALB-1392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Role:** Flammability Analyst
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Textile Seam Strength & Hydrostatic Head Permeability

- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 23±2°C; RH: 50±5%; Load cell accuracy: ±0.5% of reading; Pressure sensor accuracy: ±0.1% of full scale; Conditioning time: ≥24 hours.
